Um... on those afternoons alone at the house, I can go through quite a bit. A whole loaf of bread... peanut butter, cookies. Leftovers, ice cream... just, all in one sitting before I even really look up.\n\nDoctor: How often do these episodes of eating large amounts of food happen?\n\nPatient: I... well, I keep a log of things. Looking at that, it's been going on for many months now. It shows that nearly every week has at least one afternoon where things get out of control like that. \n\nAnd then... well, I try to make up for it afterwards. I... I'll make myself sick, or take laxatives. Or I'll just fast for a while, or push myself really hard with walking and doing chores around the house just to... to try and fix it. But yes, about once a week.\n\nDoctor: Do you worry about gaining weight after these episodes of eating?\n\nPatient: I don't... no, I haven't really noticed worrying about my weight. I'm an old man, I don't step on the scale much these days. It's not really about that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think about the possibility of gaining weight when you're considering eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: No... not really. Like I said, I don't think much about my weight. It's... it's just not something that crosses my mind when I'm eating like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think you might gain weight as a result of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Well... I suppose I do. My doctor told me I'm too thin, but... if I step on the scale and it's up even half a pound, I... I get panicky. I feel ashamed. Like I've just let myself go, and I have to... fix it right then.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... well, I keep a little log of my meals. If you look at it, for many months now... I guess nearly every week has at least one afternoon where I just... eat out of control. \n\nAnd then I have to make up for it. I... I might make myself sick, or take laxatives, or just stop eating for a while. Sometimes I just push myself really hard, walking for hours or doing heavy chores around the house to... to fix it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your fear of gaining weight influences your decision to eat large amounts of food?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. I haven't really thought about it that way. When I start eating like that, I'm not... I'm not really thinking about much of anything. It just happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you think about the possibility of gaining weight at all when you're eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I'm just... eating.\n\nDoctor: Do you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast because you're afraid of gaining weight after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... I couldn't say for sure. I just feel so terrible after I eat all that. I just... I feel like I need to get rid of it. I haven't really sat down and thought about if it's because of the scale.\n\nDoctor: Are you trying to prevent weight gain when you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... I really couldn't tell you. I'm not thinking about the future or... preventing anything. I just feel so heavy and awful in the moment. I just want that feeling to stop. That's really all it is.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I suppose I do. My doctor told me I'm too thin, but... if I step on the scale and it's up even half a pound, I... I get panicky. I feel ashamed. Like I've just let myself go, and I have to... fix it right then.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... well, I keep a little log of my meals. If you look at it, for many months now... I guess nearly every week has at least one afternoon where I just... eat out of control. \n\nAnd then I have to make up for it. I... I might make myself sick, or take laxatives, or just stop eating for a while. Sometimes I just push myself really hard, walking for hours or doing heavy chores around the house to... to fix it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your fear of gaining weight influences your decision to eat large amounts of food?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. I haven't really thought about it that way. When I start eating like that, I'm not... I'm not really thinking about much of anything. It just happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you think about the possibility of gaining weight at all when you're eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I'm just... eating.\n\nDoctor: Do you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast because you're afraid of gaining weight after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... I couldn't say for sure. I just feel so terrible after I eat all that. I just... I feel like I need to get rid of it. I haven't really sat down and thought about if it's because of the scale.\n\nDoctor: Are you trying to prevent weight gain when you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I... I really couldn't tell you. I'm not thinking about the future or... preventing anything. I just feel so heavy and awful in the moment. I just want that feeling to stop. That's really all it is.\n\nDoctor: Do you make yourself sick, take laxatives, or fast after eating because you're trying to prevent weight gain?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really know what else to tell you about it. You keep asking me that, and... I just don't know. I'm not looking at it like that. It's just... I just have to do it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your attempts to get rid of the food after eating, like making yourself sick or taking laxatives, are because you're afraid of what it might do to your weight?\n\nPatient: I suppose... I don't really like talking about it. But... my regular doctor, he tells me I'm too thin already. It's just, if I step on the scale and it's up... even just half a pound... I get... well, I panic. I feel ashamed. Like I've let myself go completely and I just have to fix it right away. So... I guess that's what you're asking.\n\nDoctor: Is your main reason for making yourself sick or taking laxatives after eating a large amount of food because you are afraid of gaining weight?\n\nPatient: I... didn't I just answer that? I don't really want to keep repeating myself. But... yes. Like I just said, even with my doctor telling me I'm already too thin... if that scale goes up even half a pound, I panic. I feel ashamed, like I've let myself go, and I just have to fix it right away. So... yes.\n\nDoctor: Is getting rid of the calories from the food the main reason you make yourself sick or take laxatives after eating a large amount of food?\n\nPatient: I thought... I thought we just went over this. Yes. It's... it's to fix it. I don't know how many different ways I can say it.\n\nDoctor: Is your fear of gaining weight the main reason you try to get rid of the food after eating a large amount?\n\nPatient: Look, doctor... I'm really getting tired of this. I've said it three times now. Yes. It's the weight. Like I told you, my doctor says I'm too thin, but... if I see I've gained even a tiny bit, I just... I panic. I feel ashamed and I have to fix it. Can we please move on from this?\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
